37:T34244,<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light-default github-dark-default" style="--shiki-light:#1f2328;--shiki-dark:#e6edf3;--shiki-light-bg:#ffffff;--shiki-dark-bg:#0d1117" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/*</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * typeset: long-form text inside a `typeset` container (docs prose, articles,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * chat messages). Three controls drive the rhythm: --typeset-size (base size),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * --typeset-leading (line height) and --typeset-flow (space between blocks).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Elements with data-slot (every library component) and anything inside</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * .not-typeset / [data-not-typeset] are left alone. A component content slot</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * that itself has `.typeset` or `data-typeset` (a tab or accordion panel, card</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * content) turns prose styles back on for its own content, inheriting the</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * surrounding preset; components nested in it stay excluded. Re-enabling works</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * three levels deep (a panel in a panel in a panel). Utilities on an element win. Presets are at the end.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">@layer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> components {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /* Defaults on a typeset root. A slot re-enabled inside prose inherits instead. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">  :is</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">.typeset</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">data-typeset</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">])</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">:not</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      :where</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">:is</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">.typeset</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">data-typeset</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]) [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">data-slot</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">], </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">:is</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">.typeset</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">data-typeset</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]) [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">data-typeset</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">])</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    ) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">  :is</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">.typeset</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">data-typeset</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /*</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   * An element is styled when its nearest boundary (a component slot or .not-typeset)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   * is a typeset root. Boundaries are B, roots T:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   *   T *            not inside T B</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   *   T B T *        not inside T B T B</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   *   T B T B T *    not inside T B T B T B</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   *   T B T B T B T *  not inside T B T B T B T B</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">      /* A wrapped chip keeps its padding, background and radius on every line. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      -webkit-box-decoration-break</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">clone</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">      /* Docs prose keeps tokens like --radius or text-ui-* on one line (no break at a</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">         hyphen); other presets wrap, and anything too long still breaks rather than overflow. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      white-space</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">var</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">--typeset-code-wrap</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">normal</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      overflow-wrap</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">: anywhere;</span></span>
